- **Intro:** Hot-Press Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Particleboard/MDF Continuous Hot-Pressing Process — Verify Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Machine Isolation: Hydraulic pressure must read 0.0 bar on the pressure gauge.
